debpalash/VoiceStudio ↗ 1 release · 1 prerelease
v0.5.8-beta.1v0.5.8-beta.1 — VoiceStudio Prerelease2026-11-08T14:50:11Z

Installer signing

Platform Signing
macOS (Apple Silicon) ✅ Signed with Developer ID and notarized by Apple
macOS (Intel) ✅ Signed with Developer ID and notarized by Apple
Windows x64 ⚠️ Unsigned: SmartScreen shows "Windows protected your PC"; choose More info → Run anyway
Windows ARM64 ⚠️ Unsigned: SmartScreen shows "Windows protected your PC"; choose More info → Run anyway
Linux x64 — Linux packages are not code-signed

Phone downloads

Android: signed APK · Play bundle.

iPhone: unsigned sideload IPA. Sign with your own Apple ID to install; this is not a TestFlight build.

AMD and Intel GPUs now speed up OmniVoice, and setup gets far more forgiving. OmniVoice GGUF runs on AMD, Intel and NVIDIA GPUs through Vulkan on Windows and Linux, dots.tts arrives on Windows with no Python setup, and a failing PyTorch download or a broken GPU no longer leaves you without a working app.

Download

Platform Installer
Windows x64 Installer
Windows ARM64 (experimental) Installer
macOS Apple Silicon DMG
macOS Intel DMG
Linux x64 AppImage · deb
Android APK · AAB
iPhone Unsigned IPA (sideload with your own Apple ID)

Upgrading: Install over your existing app; voices, projects and settings are kept.

Highlights

  • OmniVoice GGUF uses AMD, Intel and NVIDIA GPUs through Vulkan on Windows and Linux
  • dots.tts voice cloning now runs on Windows through audio.cpp, with no Python setup
  • Convert can keep your delivery, changing only the voice
  • Dubbing exports subtitles before dubbing and moves lines between speakers
  • Setup falls back to the CPU build when PyTorch downloads fail, and the app falls back to the CPU when the GPU fails

Changed

  • Account sign-in is available in every build, still off until you turn it on, and now starts on the VoiceStudio dashboard with a separate Sign up option (#3850)
  • The Pro page in the app now shows the correct $199 Lifetime price (#3831)
  • Mac releases use an Apple API key for notarization instead of an account password. (#3775) — thanks @velixio!
  • Source development now prepares the local API environment before launching Electron (#3637)
  • Remove obsolete screenshot and source-archive helpers that targeted the retired UI layout. (#3623)
  • Removed unused development tools (pytest-cov, Turborepo and a duplicate TypeScript) from the repository and fixed the remaining broken documentation links (#3571, #2603, #2903) — thanks @themrbeasley and @marciok!
  • Workflows open at /workflows framed to their steps, Calls opens at /calls, and an unknown address shows Page not found (#3547)
  • Projects lists projects under All projects with voices, transcripts, takes and exports grouped under Library; saving a dub now lives in Dubbing (#3547)
  • The OmniVoice sidecar availability check no longer imports the model, so backend startup reconcile finishes about 6 s sooner (#3534) — thanks @adrianchen91!
  • The first-run install screen is cleaner: one progress bar, a step checklist that shows the package being installed, and Cancel and the log inside the progress card (#3534) — thanks @meisolated!

Added

  • Dub, batch dub and Tools → Translate offer Kabyle (Taqbaylit) as a translation target (#3740) — thanks @Aksel103!
  • Add per-engine local model folders with authorized discovery, compatibility checks and non-destructive default reset. (#3702) — thanks @EriconYu!
  • Clone takes can be saved as MP3 as well as WAV from the save button's format menu (#3694) — thanks @lucassmonn!
  • Audiobook and Stories scripts take a per-line [emotion:...] direction (one line scared, the next excited) on IndexTTS2, CosyVoice 3 and VoxCPM2; other engines and the phone apps skip the tag instead of reading it aloud (#3664) — thanks @sidmax7!
  • Settings → Account has a Refresh button that reloads your profile and plan (#3850)
  • When the large PyTorch download keeps failing, setup now offers a much smaller CPU version so you still get a working app (#3846)
  • VoiceStudio now tests your GPU on startup and uses the CPU, with a notice, if the GPU fails (#3845)
  • Crash reports now show what was running and how much memory was free, so out-of-memory crashes are easy to spot (#3844)
  • Install and startup failures now name a cause, show the fix first and fill in their own bug report; setup warns about Smart App Control and tests Python before the large download (#3810)
  • Add an Arch Linux development source package with package-managed updates (#3771)
  • Add Android release updates through Obtainium and a publisher-operated F-Droid repository. (#3765)
  • Prepare a bundled, signed runtime and persistent folder grants for private Mac App Store validation. (#3755)
  • Allow explicit external TestFlight beta uploads while keeping internal testing as the default. (#3749)
  • Let Android and iOS users try models below the recommended RAM, with clear warnings and the existing licence and download checks. (#3738)
  • Add native Donate, Pro information and Other Apps pages to iOS and Android, with offline artwork and translated copy. (#3728)
  • Opt-in Settings switch fills [pause] markers and audiobook gaps with the take's own room tone instead of digital silence (#3615, #3611) — thanks @carmoe-prog for the idea!
  • OmniVoice GGUF uses AMD, Intel and NVIDIA GPUs through Vulkan on Windows and Linux, sizes its model to the GPU, adds a model quant picker with Download, and offers a one-time Use GPU prompt when PyTorch has no GPU (#3615, #3599) — thanks @dutch2005!
  • Galician (Galego) is now available as an app language on desktop and mobile (#3594) — thanks @B1NAR10!
  • Supermetry joins as VoiceStudio's first Strategic Sponsor, shown in SPONSORS.md, the README and the in-app Support page (#3583)
  • dots.tts now runs on Windows too: the new audio.cpp dots.tts engine clones voices in many languages with no Python setup, and Model Catalogue installs and removes each audio.cpp model on its own (#3574, #2665) — thanks @HuntingSuccubus!
  • Convert can now keep your delivery: turn on Keep my delivery to change only the voice while your timing, pauses and intonation stay as recorded (needs CosyVoice 3) (#3573, #3421)
  • A transcribed video can be exported before dubbing: subtitles of the edited transcript, or the original video with burned-in subtitles (#3555, #3393) — thanks @EntityinArray!
  • Dubbing lines can be moved to another speaker or a new speaker, one at a time or in a selection, and take that speaker's voice (#3555, #3515) — thanks @iamhrhp!
  • Audiobook scripts show a live chapter outline: click a # heading to jump there, see spoken-word counts, and insert a new chapter from the markup toolbar (#3541) — thanks @mvanhorn!
  • Urdu speech reads numbers, years, percentages and decimals in Urdu words instead of raw digits (#3540) — thanks @maatitechnologies-hue!
  • Transcribe adds an Identify speakers switch in accurate mode, with speaker labels in segments and exports (#3535) — thanks @adrianchen91!
  • Dubbing can jump between flagged transcript segments, including offscreen rows, with Previous and Next issue controls (#3535) — thanks @Thakay!
  • Voice Cloning's Emotion button is always on the composer and adapts to the engine — sliders on IndexTTS2, a written direction on CosyVoice 3 and VoxCPM2, and a clear explanation elsewhere; Insert menus gray out tags the engine would read aloud (#3422)
  • Nix / NixOS users can build and run VoiceStudio from source with the new community-maintained flake (#2961) — thanks @shamil2!
  • Opper is available as a named LLM provider for translation and rewriting, used only after you select it and add a key (#2668) — thanks @Felixkw12!
  • API Route is now available as a named LLM provider for translation and rewriting, used only after you select it and add a key (#2596) — thanks @DennyHo0917!

Fixed

  • Linux builds no longer drop the Vulkan GPU module at random when the header check races the compiler (#3866)
  • The Model Catalogue licence dialog now lists every licence an engine needs, so one acceptance covers all of them (#3863) — thanks @InesZenkri!
  • Desktop updates download only the changed parts of the installer on Windows, macOS and the Linux AppImage instead of the whole app, and the offered size no longer counts every installer in the release (#3861)
  • Updated pypdf, pyasn1, msgpack, nltk, hydra-core and litellm to releases that fix published security advisories (#3857)
  • mlx-audio Kokoro now lists its preset voices and speaks with a voice of the requested language instead of always using af_heart (#3833) — thanks @teehihi!
  • Verbalize Portuguese clock times (14h30) and Brazilian currency (R$ 1.250,00) before synthesis (#3832) — thanks @vanshsingh3711-wq, and @iurymagano for the report!
  • Fixed 21 dubbing, subtitle, dictation, TTS, translation and download bugs from the November community batch (#3819) — thanks @rudycelekli!
  • VoxCPM2 now runs on CPU instead of failing on GPUs its PyTorch build no longer supports, such as the GTX 10 series (#3792) — thanks @vandyahmad24!
  • Install matching Linux ROCm Torch for supported sidecars and report their effective AMD routing (#3787) — thanks @dutch2005!
  • Model install time estimates move to the next unit when rounding up reaches it (#3763) — thanks @kevin9327!
  • Preserve dub separation failure diagnostics, including interrupted progress, while redacting private paths (#3762) — thanks @DPhngNam!
  • Byte sizes in Settings move to the next unit when rounding reaches 1024, so you no longer see "1024 MB" (#3733) — thanks @kevin9327!
  • Backend deaths from an external kill (exit code 4294967295 / -1) are now explained as a termination from outside the app instead of a bare crash code (#3727) — thanks @meutex!
  • Relative times in the voice library move to the next unit when rounding reaches it, so you no longer see "60 minutes ago" (#3719) — thanks @kevin9327!
  • On Windows, crash reports, account settings and the PyTorch build choice are saved reliably even while antivirus is scanning the file (#3708) — thanks @samay-hash!
  • Dictation now ends a Chinese or Japanese sentence with its full stop when it closes on a bracket or quote (#3703) — thanks @kevin9327!
  • Clarify first-run app, runtime and model setup and show platform-specific disk requirements. (#3700) — thanks @EriconYu!
  • OmniVoice dubs into Arabic, Chinese and Kurdish instead of stopping with "Choose a supported language" after translation (#3692, #3651) — thanks @hkarmoush!
  • OpenAI-compatible transcriptions no longer fail with HTTP 500 when MLX Whisper reports NaN or infinite segment scores (#3678) — thanks @filip-gooddata!
  • /health names the device the active engine generates on (vulkan, cuda, rocm, xpu, mps or cpu) and the engine itself, without reporting an unverified native device as cpu (#3675) — thanks @dutch2005!
  • Require administrator authorization for remote editing, deleting and importing of stored voices, projects and other content while paired phones keep running generations (#3670) — thanks @DeryFerd!
  • Require administrator authorization for remote gallery imports, edits and deletions (#3669) — thanks @DeryFerd!
  • Explicit num_step and guidance_scale unsupported by the engine, or steps outside its documented range, now get a 400 naming the parameter instead of silently altered audio (#3654, #3667) — thanks @swadhinbiswas!
  • Voice profiles whose reference clip the OmniVoice GGUF runtime cannot read (a non-WAV, or a WAV in a sample format it does not decode) now clone instead of failing the spawn (#3655, #3666) — thanks @RerankerGuo!
  • The setup GPU check no longer tells NVIDIA users to update a driver that already qualifies; it names the CPU or ROCm PyTorch build instead (#3657) — thanks @ColinCee!
  • English keeps a space-grouped number like 10 000 whole instead of speaking "ten" and leaving "000" behind (#3656) — thanks @kevin9327!
  • Windows Smart App Control and other launch blocks during engine setup now name the blocked program instead of a bare "spawn UNKNOWN" (#3652) — thanks @ColinCee!
  • Keep native Vulkan GPU discovery working when hidden Windows full reports hang, and require the Windows Vulkan release payload. (#3648) — thanks @dutch2005!
  • Diarisation models that need licence acceptance, such as Sortformer, now show the licence button so you can accept it (#3642, #3647) — thanks @hkarmoush!
  • Source startup now explains when Node.js is too old and how to upgrade before setup or Electron downloads (#3854) — thanks @velixio!
  • Signing out of your VoiceStudio account always forgets it on this device, even offline (#3850)
  • Setup keeps the install folder you choose, even if it already holds files, instead of going back to the system drive (#3847)
  • AMD cards that need a remap now work on Linux from the first run, and a ROCm install stays selected across launches and updates (#3843)
  • Dubbing no longer turns an unlisted language tag into a different language: Filipino stays Filipino, Estonian stays Estonian, and unknown tags stay unknown instead of becoming Finnish or Spanish (#3834) — thanks @teehihi!
  • Creating workflows and other content no longer fails when VoiceStudio is opened over plain HTTP on a LAN (#3827) — thanks @mertkayacs!
  • Setup detects stalled downloads, offers diagnostic logs and support links, and allows more time for large package downloads. (#3785)
  • Android voice cloning shows progress and keeps Cancel visible while protecting the selected recording (#3782)
  • Backend launch tests model supported hosts explicitly so Intel Mac packaging can finish its checks (#3754)
  • Mac App Store packages use the bundle identifier registered for VoiceStudio Pro for Mac (#3753)
  • Phone model downloads resume with background transfers, parallel ranges, automatic retries, and visible percentage, speed and time remaining (#3752)
  • Fix iOS store upload by using a Share extension identifier accepted by App Store validation. (#3744)
  • Fix iOS store export by signing the workspace widget with its registered App ID. (#3743)
  • Fix iOS store signing with the registered Share extension and App Group, and bundle required privacy declarations. (#3742)
  • When the backend never answers and prints nothing, the error now quotes the backend's own log from that launch, so you can see where startup stalled (#3724) — thanks @meutex!
  • Restored mobile Speech and Gallery, expanded offline voice models, and skipped runtime CI for documentation/test-only changes. (#3715)
  • Moving the data folder to a new location no longer fails with "identity_unavailable" on Linux, where a same-tick folder check could not see its own change (#3706) — thanks @samay-hash!
  • ASR engine rows show localized setup guidance, including NeMo’s installation warning, and name Settings → Models (#3643)
  • Startup reports blocked Python executables without automatically reinstalling intact runtimes (#3640)
  • Fix Windows installer process detection using CIM executable paths. (#3636)
  • Safely move app data into existing empty folders or mounted drives, rejecting nested destinations and preserving unrelated files during rollback (#3631) — thanks @DeryFerd!
  • Use a searchable, keyboard-accessible preset voice picker with themed menus (#3630) — thanks @DeryFerd!
  • Handle native process exit races, preserve network error diagnostics and stabilize deferred UI cleanup and screenshot-test sheet transitions. (#3629)
  • Prevent spurious permission errors when timed-out operations finish cleanup on macOS. (#3626)
  • Keep portrait search results available and disabled while another portrait is saving. (#3625)
  • Prevent Windows updates from mistaking sibling folders or the installer for a running app. (#3624) — thanks @majalali83!
  • Keep regional model downloads eligible for automatic endpoint failover and reject mirrors without usable download metadata. (#3622)
  • Prevent startup crashes from incorrectly encoded environment files. (#3621)
  • CosyVoice and the desktop bridge pick up hydra-core 1.3.8 and rustls 0.23.46; installed CosyVoice asks for one repair and keeps its model (#3615, #3597, #3598)
  • Android phones download models through hf-mirror.com CDN subdomains like iPhones do, and both refuse redirects to non-standard ports (#3615, #3609) — thanks @DeryFerd!
  • The backend no longer fails to start on Windows when a system tool prints characters the locale can't decode (#3615, #3612) — thanks @hachmit87-lab for the report!
  • The Galician interface now includes the expressive speech strings, which fixes the web build on main (#3608)
  • A voice saved or locked from a long take no longer repeats a word at the start of every chunk: takes keep their whole text, script markup such as [pause] never reaches a reference transcript, and voices saved earlier from a cut take transcribe their clip instead (#3062, #3602)
  • Radeon GPUs missing from a fixed remap table (RX 9070 on a gfx1200-only ROCm build, gfx1152, gfx1035) run on the GPU, and the setup GPU check names the installed PyTorch build and the ROCm opt-in instead of saying Windows has no ROCm build (#3418, #3602)
  • An audio.cpp runtime install on Windows no longer fails with "access denied" while antivirus scans the freshly checked binary (#2685, #3602)
  • The backend keeps answering while it frees an idle model's memory, instead of showing "not responding" (#3558, #3595)
  • First-run setup lets you continue once a different TTS engine is installed and selected, without the default model pack (#3581, #3595)
  • Dubbing's Cast picker always offers each speaker's Auto voice extracted from the video, so an override can be undone (#3582, #3595)
  • IndexTTS honours the Speed setting through its native duration control, and /ws/tts accepts a duration_factor (#3588, #3595)
  • An Intel Mac running from source now shows the remote-backend screen instead of an endless "Python environment is incomplete" error, and bun run setup:api explains why it cannot install (#3584, #3595)
  • The speech CLI keeps uploads well-formed for any file name, accepts numeric loopback addresses such as 127.1, and never sends local requests through a proxy (#3594) — thanks @rudycelekli!
  • English speech reads a 24-hour time on the hour as "nineteen hundred" instead of "nineteen o'clock" (#3594) — thanks @kevin9327!
  • Source checkouts on macOS 14 and older start with bun run dev again instead of failing on a missing lockf (#3594) — thanks @sebas-rodri!
  • Reopening a designed voice saved with seed 0 keeps that seed instead of picking a new one (#3590)
  • With the Auto download region, setup now uses the Aliyun PyPI mirror when a quick one-wheel speed check finds it faster than PyPI, so users in China get the mirror without choosing it (#3576, #3444)
  • CosyVoice 3 installed from source now gets its system prompt for directed, cloned and cross-lingual takes, matching the managed engine (#3575)
  • Dubbed lines now start on detected speech, so a sigh, breath or footstep before a line no longer makes it start early, and trailing breaths no longer pad its slot (#3573, #3419)
  • VoxCPM2 generates again on NVIDIA GPUs when it runs in the main engine, instead of failing every time with a CUDA graph assertion (#3571, #3567) — thanks @Fguarien!
  • The OmniVoice GGUF engine now works out of the box: Windows, Apple Silicon and Linux installers ship its native runtime (#3569, #2780) — thanks @DestinyQJY and @chiarorosa!
  • OmniVoice on an older NVIDIA GPU that the installed PyTorch has no kernels for now shows as running on the CPU and gets the CPU time allowance, so slow renders are not cut short (#3565, #3067)
  • When Windows Smart App Control, an application control policy or an antivirus blocks one of the engine's files, the error names the blocker and VoiceStudio no longer re-downloads the runtime (#3557, #3209, #3224) — thanks @ark2006aug13-byte and @pauloerv-sketch!
  • First-run setup downloads large wheels such as the Windows CUDA torch over several connections and resumes them after Cancel or Retry, instead of crawling at about 1 MB/s on networks that limit each connection (#3557, #3411, #3060, #3202) — thanks @justcallme-A and @hadialshaer!
  • The app no longer crashes with an "insertBefore" error when browser page translation or an extension changes the page (#3555, #3168) — thanks @wwwfelipe29-ai!
  • An engine install that needs a C++ compiler, such as CosyVoice 3, now says which build tools to install instead of blaming the network (#3553, #3423)
  • Switching Linux to the AMD ROCm build of PyTorch finishes setup instead of failing on a Pillow version conflict (#3553, #3158) — thanks @Blade-pt and @prashker!
  • Older NVIDIA GPUs that the installed PyTorch has no kernels for (such as Maxwell cards) now show as running on the CPU instead of "CUDA ready" (#3553, #3067) — thanks @BheeshamKS!
  • Voice cloning on Mac no longer fails with "couldn't synthesize audio" after a clone without reference text (#3552, #3551) — thanks @macfluryy!
  • Opening Calls after visiting Integrations no longer crashes the Calls page (#3550)
  • Gallery switches sections in the page header, Community gains search and lists each voice once, and the saved-voice list highlights only the current voice (#3547)
  • The navigation keeps one layout on every page, the bottom player no longer covers the nav rail, and the Pro page header clears the account corner (#3547)
  • Command search finds pages by short names such as clone, models, integrations, pro and dark, and lists recent takes once with their age and length (#3547)
  • Script samples and placeholders in every language show literal [pause 0.5s] and [voice:NAME] markup, and uncast characters get one calm note instead of warnings (#3547)
  • Interrupted Stories and Audiobook renders show once per project on their own page, explain a disabled Resume, and can be dismissed (#3547)
  • Every workspace shows the same not-ready notice with one Set up action, and disabled buttons now say why (#3547)
  • Audiobook and Dubbing stay usable in short windows: the script editor and segment list keep their room, and find, stage and readiness notes share one slim strip (#3547)
  • The Hearth light theme uses a non-red colour for primary buttons, keeping red for destructive actions (#3546)
  • Settings opens on General, page names match their contents, and the breadcrumb shows the model family (#3546)
  • Settings → Credentials has one Save per translation provider, and Settings Save buttons enable only after a change (#3546)
  • Model rows name their licence type, show sizes in one unit style, and drop the duplicate Selected button (#3546)
  • Performance presets no longer switch Apple Silicon transcription from MLX to CPU Faster-Whisper (#3546)
  • Model packs preselect the largest pack that fits your free disk space instead of Max (#3546)
  • Settings → Storage shows one low-disk warning, and the disk bar turns amber or red when nearly full (#3546)
  • Settings search lists only matching pages and finds Theme by "dark" or "light" and model families by name (#3546)
  • Settings → Usage no longer says VoiceStudio has no analytics; optional analytics is controlled in Privacy (#3546)
  • Settings → Updates "What's new" shows formatted release notes instead of raw markdown tables and links (#3546)
  • Job progress history repairs duplicate event numbers so replayed progress never skips or repeats a step (#3542) — thanks @sedatdagg!
  • Long transcriptions no longer repeat themselves, and the transcription model stays loaded between requests (#3542) — thanks @gifmac!
  • The OmniVoice GGUF runtime builds for every x86-64 CPU, and a runtime that hits an illegal instruction is reported clearly (#3542) — thanks @inteligencia66!
  • Streamed generations and long-form renders stay connected behind proxies such as Cloudflare while a slow chunk or chapter renders (#3542) — thanks @fouadSalkini!
  • bun run start and bun run preview launch the desktop app again on a fresh checkout (#3542) — thanks @inteligencia66!
  • Turning off Tailscale sharing removes only VoiceStudio's own share and never resets other apps' Tailscale serve settings (#3542) — thanks @hiiqbiz-wq!
  • Saved Voice Design profiles preserve natural-language prompts across reopening, previewing, and portable export/import (#3541) — thanks @XiaoPuOuO!
  • Audiobook and Stories renders keep cached chapters during overlapping resumes, stay responsive while preparing the cache, and remove incomplete outputs when assembly fails or is cancelled (#3540) — thanks @Bortlesboat!
  • GPU acceleration report names a card the installed PyTorch build has no kernels for, instead of reporting a CUDA start that would work and pointing at a driver update that cannot add them (#3540) — thanks @Faisal-Fayaz!
  • Long text between [pause] markers is split into sentence chunks like unmarked text, so a long passage next to a pause no longer degrades into garbled audio (#3540) — thanks @maatitechnologies-hue!
  • Long Urdu, Persian, Arabic and Hindi scripts split at sentence ends and clause marks instead of mid-sentence, even with no space after the mark (#3540) — thanks @maatitechnologies-hue!
  • File transcription no longer cancels when you leave the Transcribe page; the result is saved and a toast links back to it (#3535) — thanks @adrianchen91!
  • The dubbing timeline stays visible when a transcript cue is selected, including in long transcripts and compact windows (#3535) — thanks @BichengWang!
  • MLX-Audio OuteTTS voice cloning reads the reference clip with a Whisper model installed from Model Catalogue instead of hanging on a download, and reuses the voice on later generations (#3535) — thanks @kevin9327 for the fix and @unovlad for the report!
  • Space-grouped thousands like 1 234 or 10 000 keep their digits in French, German, Russian and other comma-decimal languages (#3535) — thanks @NishchayMahor!
  • Bug reports now redact a nonstandard home folder even when punctuation follows it (#3534) — thanks @zichen0116!
  • Updated DOMPurify and proxy-addr to versions that fix published security advisories (#3534) — thanks @anupamme!
  • Voice Cloning sends manually entered reference transcripts with saved voices instead of dropping them before synthesis (#3534) — thanks @isnaenihidayat!
  • Convert no longer reads short non-Latin clips (e.g. Armenian) as the wrong language: pick the source language, or it follows the target voice's language (#3534) — thanks @YuraZakaryan!
  • Desktop releases now include Android and iPhone sideload downloads from the same version, without Apple signing blocking the iPhone file (#3516)
  • Prevent reset and uninstall cleanup from deleting data through lookalike or linked folders. (#3512)
  • Error banners show diagnosed problems in your language with a button that fixes them, such as installing a speech-to-text model, freeing memory or opening Storage (#3511)
  • A running Story or Audiobook render stays visible after you leave its page: the other book page and the notifications bell show its progress with Show progress and Stop, and interrupted Stories renders can be resumed (#3510)
  • Source checkouts with a missing Python environment get Retry instead of a folder picker that could not work, and installed apps never show the developer setup command (#3508)
  • A finished runtime setup no longer offers "Resume interrupted download", and Resume reuses the existing environment and download cache instead of fetching PyTorch again (#3508)
  • Windows runtime refreshes retry and update files in place when OneDrive, antivirus or a running backend locks them, and a lasting lock now says which program to close (#3507)
  • Windows setup that crashed with exit code 3221225477 on an outdated Visual C++ runtime now names the cause and tells you to update the Microsoft Visual C++ Redistributable (#3507)
  • Russian labels read naturally in Export, Audiobook, About and seed controls (RAM, stems, cover, seed) (#3479) — thanks @al2m!
  • Caption imports keep ruby base words without speaking pronunciation guides or losing later cues (#3360) — thanks @014-code!
  • Percentages now speak the trailing zeros that were written, so 2.50% is two point five zero percent (#3359) — thanks @kevin9327!
  • On Apple Silicon, the Quality and Max model packs install and use Whisper on the GPU (MLX) instead of a CPU-only copy (#3349) — thanks @bee-interactive!
  • Locking a take keeps its whole text as the voice's reference transcript, instead of cutting it at 100 characters (#3320) — thanks @bercedev!
  • Markdown bold and italic markers are stripped before synthesis instead of being read aloud as "asterisk" (#3197, #3205) — thanks @swadhinbiswas!
  • Cmd/Ctrl+Z in a Dub page field such as the glossary, translation notes or a segment's Start/End now undoes your typing there instead of the last transcript edit, and Cmd/Ctrl+S on a timeline segment no longer snaps it to the nearest onset (#3199) — thanks @HEOJUNFO!
  • MOSS-TTS-Nano reuses the configured tokenizer cache instead of nesting another cache inside its model code and exceeding Windows path limits (#3137, #3167) — thanks @ZFzuofeng!
  • Startup no longer loads unused OmniVoice weights when another speech engine is selected (#3127) — thanks @isnaenihidayat!
  • Smart Fit keeps overlapping speakers and captions on one timeline without replaying shared video (#3008) — thanks @jonathanwxh-cell!
  • Dubbing segment Start and End fields no longer round the time when you tab through them without typing (#2986) — thanks @HEOJUNFO!
  • Model downloads show honest overall progress instead of a bar that jumps backwards or under-reports while several files are in flight (#2936, #2952) — thanks @DeryFerd!
  • Local marketplace installs keep designed voices and their saved design settings (#2878) — thanks @agammann!
  • CosyVoice's one-click install now resolves on Linux ARM64, with or without an NVIDIA GPU (#2830) — thanks @Jason69628723!
  • The macOS menu-bar icon now uses a correctly sized monochrome glyph that follows the system appearance (#2750, #2828) — thanks @Fredy002 and @windery!
  • On macOS, the app icon is no longer oversized in the Dock, Launchpad and Finder (#2744, #2749) — thanks @windery!
  • Intel Arc (XPU) GPUs load models on Windows and recover from out-of-memory errors instead of failing every later request until a restart (#2747) — thanks @nur-zaman!
  • Settings → System reports an Ascend NPU host's device name and VRAM, and the memory-flush snapshot reads the NPU and Intel XPU (#2582) — thanks @li-lizhe!

License

cua-driver-rs-v0.44.0cua-driver-rs: v0.44.0 Prerelease2026-11-08T14:49:11Z

Cua Driver 0.44.0

Summary

This release adds 2 features and includes 7 fixes.

Features

  • capture the KWin desktop through ScreenShot2 behind kwin-capture. (#6890) Thanks @astrovm, @shellwen.
  • write the KWin desktop entry for the install that owns the CLI. (#6836) Thanks @astrovm.

Fixes

  • report macOS opened-window identity per action. (#6928) Thanks @lidge-jun.
  • type exact text on the active keyboard layout or refuse it. (#6827) Thanks @astrovm.
  • name the dialog that blocks keys and how to reach a spreadsheet cell (CUA-3763). (#6937)
  • clear portal-input clippy lints in platform-linux. (#6940)
  • renumber Linux list_windows z_index after its filters. (#6938)
  • call Text Input Sources only with the thread affinity macOS requires (CUA-3763). (#6939)
  • cover foreground activation enabling a macOS AppKit control. (#6947)

Contributors

Thanks to @astrovm, @lidge-jun, @shellwen for contributing to this release.

Install

macOS and Linux:

/bin/bash -c "$(curl -fsSL https://cua.ai/driver/install.sh)"

Windows:

irm https://cua.ai/driver/install.ps1 | iex

Cua Driver ships universal macOS binaries, x86_64 and arm64 Windows builds, and Linux preview builds. Release assets also include tag-pinned installer and uninstaller scripts.

Why GitHub says “Pre-release”

GitHub's label is used only to keep this monorepo's repository-wide “Latest” pointer from switching between independently released products. A plain Cua Driver SemVer is a stable release; npm and PyPI publish it on their normal stable channels, and the installers resolve the versioned cua-driver-rs-v* releases directly.

Cua Driver 0.43.3-nightly.20261108.42446386787 (nightly)

Automated immutable nightly built from an exact main commit. Nightlies are opt-in, may be less stable than regular releases, and never replace stable update discovery.

cua-driver-rs-v0.43.2cua-driver-rs: v0.43.2 Prerelease2026-11-08T03:28:01Z

Cua Driver 0.43.2

Summary

This release includes 3 fixes.

Fixes

  • name the no-window case in turn evidence (fixes Linux release gate). (#6839)
  • retry perception resolve after extension lock contention. (#6844)
  • sweep orphaned extension staging and clean it on every refusal. (#6845)

Performance

  • batch macOS type_text key events and stop polling a field that never changes. (#6884) Thanks dry.
  • batch type_text key events on Windows and Linux. (#6892)

Contributors

Thanks to dry for contributing to this release.

Install

macOS and Linux:

/bin/bash -c "$(curl -fsSL https://cua.ai/driver/install.sh)"

Windows:

irm https://cua.ai/driver/install.ps1 | iex

Cua Driver ships universal macOS binaries, x86_64 and arm64 Windows builds, and Linux preview builds. Release assets also include tag-pinned installer and uninstaller scripts.

Why GitHub says “Pre-release”

GitHub's label is used only to keep this monorepo's repository-wide “Latest” pointer from switching between independently released products. A plain Cua Driver SemVer is a stable release; npm and PyPI publish it on their normal stable channels, and the installers resolve the versioned cua-driver-rs-v* releases directly.

hugohe3/ppt-master ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v6.9.0 Stable2026-11-08T10:38:28Z

Image to PPTX gains OCR text evidence, text-removed bases, and checked image layers, and now runs on any vision host with an image_gen.py reference-image backend. Narration adds an OpenAI Realtime provider, image_gen.py can produce animated GIFs on OpenAI gpt-image-2.6, and a round of conversion and round-trip fixes stops sources from being overwritten or silently altered.

Image to PPTX

  • New scripts/image_ocr.py: scan OCRs normalized page images into reviewable text blocks (line boxes, estimated font size, sampled ink color), and the text layer becomes native text over a text-removed base. Removal results are composited back onto source pixels, and merged bases are checked for text left inside removal boxes (37efd014, e55a56ff, 574265a0, d1619e4f).
  • New scripts/image_layers.py: check verifies an extracted layer is registered against the page, diff-matte derives a layer from the page's own pixels, mono-matte cuts a single-color graphic (such as a white logo) cleanly from a key plate, and merges are layer-aware and honour occlusion (bbc84447, 3c5cd655, 4a68cdda, 79d7cf57).
  • The profile is no longer tied to one host: any vision host with a reference-image path works, either host-native editing or image_gen.py --reference-image (OpenAI, Gemini). Every page follows one assembly path; text removal defaults to -b openai and registered layers to -b gemini, with at most two residue retries before a page is marked for manual work (9577d604, 09547b1a, 7387d7b9, 6b0a2a5a, 8e809511).

AI image generation

  • image_gen.py --gif-frames N [--gif-frame-ms MS] returns an animated GIF from OpenAI gpt-image-2.6-*, for new images or to animate an existing one with --reference-image. Frame sizes are fitted to the server's sprite-sheet grids, which were measured on every frame count (db3bfc13).
  • gpt-image-2.6-* models get the same size and quality handling as the 2.5 family (7c0eb65d).
  • Each image is written once in its filename's format: a .jpg item keeps Gemini's JPEG bytes unchanged instead of a PNG round trip, and no orphan .png is left behind (8fe777ae).
  • In manifest runs, rate limits go straight to the runner, which halves concurrency and honours Retry-After on the first 429 instead of after four backend retries. Successes are persisted from the worker thread, and each completion line reports the delivered pixels (9828c101, 54385b11, 650b5838, 946241c6).
  • Sheet slicing respects sheets that carry their own transparency, source sheets are protected from being overwritten, and 16-bit grayscale images convert correctly (8db9dc57, 75e1fbf5, ce27cae0).
  • New AI Image Generation page records measured cost, size limits, text rendering, editing, and GIF behavior of the OpenAI and Gemini models (a99224bc).

Narration

  • notes_to_audio.py --provider openai uses gpt-realtime-2.1-mini over the Realtime WebSocket (the replacement OpenAI names for tts-1 and gpt-4o-mini-tts, which retire on 2027-01-06). Each page confirms the effective session config before accepting audio. Recording-based custom voices work with --voice-id voice_... (ec4ca8bc, c67ca5fa, 4c449a11, 82468e8d).

Edit Native PPTX round trip

  • An export -o that points at the source or originally imported PPTX is refused before anything is written; previously it replaced the source, and later exports failed the source fingerprint (1d31b9ea).
  • Edited native objects stay faithful on export (9b050352).

Source conversion (source_to_md)

  • Output paths that resolve or hardlink to an input are refused, so a pre-existing alias can no longer overwrite a source DOCX/XLSX; same-title web pages in one batch get distinct files (087cb942).
  • PDF: soft masks, rotated table text, and table cell order are kept; header and footer bands are classified in displayed page coordinates (7d1fdd7c, 719786f2, 545e7fe9).
  • DOCX and Pandoc: resource links, imports, and text stay faithful, and Pandoc includes resolve from the source with their media (21437a53, 1123e729). Charts keep their category context and literal Office text (fc627fee).

Templates, icons, and the live preview editor

  • Re-installing a template replaces its Installed-from line instead of stacking another (edc204a8).
  • Each embedded icon instance gets its own internal IDs, so repeated icons no longer collide (1d35572c).
  • The SVG editor keeps typed keys in form fields, shows raster data-URI images, and verifies that a running server is its own before reusing or stopping it (afad284d, afbfcd10).

Community

  • ModelGo joins as a corporate sponsor, and the README states that only the tool is free, not model tokens (f7fd3393, 263525c3, 2e8238a5, 9c39bf37).

Yeachan-Heo/oh-my-codex ↗ 3 releases · 3 stable
v0.22.1 Stable2026-11-08T08:52:29Z

oh-my-codex v0.22.1

Summary

This patch release ships critical fixes for the omx question command when stdin is closed, adds readline close event handling for EOF detection, reapplies verified handoffs and safe drains after v0.22.0, accepts aliased Codex homes for external live pins, and addresses three Codex findings from the v0.22.0 release review.

Fixed

  • ACCEPT ALIASED CODEX HOMES FOR EXTERNAL LIVE PINS (#3939): Accept either spelling of the configured Codex home when validating live pin cache paths, enabling external Codex home links and symlinks to resolve correctly while maintaining no-follow traversal security. This fixes live pin resolution when Codex homes are aliased or redirected.
  • FIX EOF DETECTION FOR CLOSED STDIN IN OMX QUESTION --UI (#4048, #4046): Replaced input stream listeners with readline close event for more reliable EOF detection when stdin is closed. Handle closed stdin in omx question --ui with proper error handling to prevent crashes when the command is invoked without stdin.
  • PRESERVE NON-FORCE DRAINS AND REQUIRE VERIFIED CODE HANDOFF (#4034): Non-force team drains no longer interrupt in-flight worker Git operations, and worker code handoff to the leader requires verified evidence before integration. This change was held out of v0.22.0.
  • ADDRESS CODEX FINDINGS FROM v0.22.0 RELEASE REVIEW (#4047): Fixes for issues identified in Codex review of release PR #4042. Herdr team shutdown now fails closed when workspace teardown fails, so team state and worktrees are preserved instead of removed while workers may still run. Scale-up honors a team's persisted Herdr launch mode, and startup readiness evidence is tracked per worker instead of leaking across concurrent startups.

Compatibility

No breaking API changes. Release-coupled metadata (package.json, package-lock.json, Cargo workspace, and plugin metadata) all report 0.22.1 at the pinned source.

Verification

  • npm ci — passed; packages installed and lifecycle hooks completed.
  • npm run build — passed.
  • npm test — the local run did not complete, so no local result is claimed. The full CI suite passed on dev 108bea31 (the source of this release), CI run 42458800471.
  • npm run lint — passed. No lint fixes required.
  • npm run check:no-unused — passed. No unused imports or declarations.

Contributors

Thanks to @ev78394 (#3939), @shenyankm (#4034), and @Yeachan-Heo for contributing to this release, and to @iqdoctor for reporting #4043.

v0.22.0 Stable2026-11-08T05:25:08Z

oh-my-codex v0.22.0

Summary

This minor release ships the Herdr team runtime backend Phase 2 with worker capacity management and task claim renewal, improves conflict handling in team operations to fail closed on Git conflicts, and adds session execution comparisons and regression gates for reliable task tracking during multi-agent workflows.

Added

  • HERDR TEAM RUNTIME BACKEND PHASE 2 (#4006): Implement the Herdr team runtime backend continuation, including native session pane coordination, detached-launch design notes, and improved tmux integration patterns. This solidifies the team runtime foundation with enhanced reliability and design clarity.
  • SESSION EXECUTION COMPARISONS AND REGRESSION GATES (#3915): Add omx session compare <baseline> <candidate> command with --fail-on-regression flag to capture execution snapshots during session transitions, compare sessions against baselines, and gate further work when regressions are detected (exit 2 for regression, 3 for inconclusive). This enables robust regression detection during multi-agent workflows and task execution validation.

Fixed

  • RESERVE WORKER CAPACITY, RENEW TASK CLAIMS, AND GATE DELIVERY COMPLETION (#4036): Fix followup bounds, implement claim renewal, gate delivery success, close lease gaps, recover pending receipts, bound lease shutdown, surface renewal errors, and recognize terminal handoffs. Support system Bash and renewal on tool activity; fix scope root reactivation to delivery debt and renew leases despite heartbeat persistence failures. Reserve worker capacity before assignment and compare persisted assignment snapshots for validation.
  • FAIL CLOSED ON GIT CONFLICTS AND HAND OFF PERSISTED TASK CLAIMS (#4035): Remove automatic conflict resolution strategies (-X theirs/-X ours), make conflicts visible and require manual resolution, preserve conflict state during integration retries, align task assignment protocol with leader's atomic preclaim, and expand conflict-scenario test coverage.

Compatibility

No breaking API changes. Release-coupled metadata (package.json, package-lock.json, Cargo workspace, and plugin metadata) all report 0.22.0 at the pinned source.

Verification

  • PR #4042 CI: 64 checks passed (one transient macOS network failure in native build).
  • Back-merge PR #4044 CI: 61 checks passed (one transient macOS network failure in native build).

Contributors

Thanks to @salacoste for the Herdr team runtime backend Phase 2 (#4006), @shenyankm for conflict handling improvements (#4035, #4036), and @hiSandog for session execution comparisons and regression gates (#3915).

v0.21.48 Stable2026-11-07T21:47:40Z

oh-my-codex v0.21.48

Summary

This patch release stores live Codex pins outside the plugin fingerprint root so live-pin writes no longer change the fingerprint Codex approved (#4010, fixes #3898), and removes role-based reasoning-effort defaults from role prompts (#4021), ensuring Vision stays extraction-only unless reassessment is justified.

Fixed

  • LIVE PINS OUTSIDE CODEX PLUGIN FINGERPRINT ROOT (#4010, fixes #3898): OMX wrote mutable live-pin files inside Codex's fingerprinted plugin-version directory, so every pin write changed the plugin fingerprint and Codex repeatedly invalidated hook approval. Live pins now live in <cacheBase>/.omx-pins/<version>, outside the fingerprint root, so pin writes no longer change the approved plugin fingerprint (reviewer: @iqdoctor; reporter: @AkiraLan).
  • ROLE-BASED REASONING-EFFORT DEFAULTS (#4020, #4021): #4021 replaced fixed 'Default effort: low/medium' role defaults in seven role prompts (build-fixer, debugger, git-master, style-reviewer, team-executor, vision, writer) with task-based effort recommendations that preserve the leader's selected effort. Vision prompt is updated to make effort recommendations conditional: 'If reassessment is justified from task demands, remaining uncertainty, available proof, and resource limits, recommend a supported reasoning effort and briefly compare plausible alternatives without a role-based default; otherwise preserve extraction-only output.' This ensures all role prompts correctly defer to leader effort selection and documented success criteria.

Compatibility

No breaking API changes. Release-coupled metadata was synchronized to 0.21.48 on dev by #4017. package.json, both root package-lock.json version entries, the Cargo workspace and six workspace crates, and plugin metadata all report 0.21.48 at the pinned source.

Verification

  • npm ci — passed; 172 packages installed and lifecycle hooks completed.
  • npm run build — passed.
  • npm test — passed on the full test run: runtime build, all Node test files, generated catalog check, and prompt-inventory check completed.
  • npm run lint — passed across 900 files. Biome emitted the existing linter.rules.recommended deprecation notice; no lint fixes were applied.
  • Release workflow 42416497142 completed successfully with native builds for macOS arm64/x64, Linux x64, and portable bundles.

Contributors

Thanks to @clawdbot, @dependabot[bot], @AkiraLan, @ev78394, @iqdoctor, monitor-watch, and @Yeachan-Heo for contributing to this release.

redhat-et/ripwire ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.6.10 Stable2026-11-08T08:24:26Z

[0.6.10] — 2026-11-08

Seventy-one merged pull requests since 0.6.9 (#433 to #570). What a user sees (each entry below states its own measurement and its floors):

  • More complete answers: on our fixed in-sample questions (the same 163 everyday, 39 multi-file and 56 large-repository questions, commands and grader as the README answers table, one call each, no cache), everyday answers complete 129 → 132 of 163 (27 partial, 4 none), multi-file 12 → 19 of 39, large repositories 23 → 30 of 56; required items delivered 1,142 → 1,152, 84 → 114 and 148 → 164; no everyday answer stated something wrong (read in source). Answers are larger: everyday answers total 2.37 MB against 1.95 MB (median 10.7 KB against 10.6 KB), multi-file 722 KB against 536 KB, large repositories 1.97 MB against 1.38 MB, so bytes per complete answer rise from 15.1 to 18.0 KB (everyday) and from 60 to 66 KB (large repositories). One everyday question that read complete in 0.6.9 reads none in 0.6.10 (textual-09; hono-17 stays complete under the rule for exports flagged as public API; the README footnote says why). A grader input lost in a cleanup was restored (GRADER-FIX-1107), so the 0.6.9 and 0.6.10 columns are graded by the same scripts. In-sample questions, so progress between releases, not held-out performance; measured on main just before #570 (the --for anchor-choice change), which these figures do not include.
  • Faster and lighter, as percent changes (each figure is that pull request's own measurement against the build just before it, on one machine; the changes were not measured together, and absolute seconds and megabytes come with the 0.7.0 table, measured on one other machine):
    • Warm --deps and --for on large trees use 35 to 67 % less CPU: vscode --deps -67 %, cockroach --for -57 %, envoy --deps -35 % (#542); a warm TypeScript --deps uses a further 22 % less CPU on vscode (#554). Cold runs gain less: instructions -14 % on vscode, -12 % on cockroach, -4 % on envoy.
    • Peak memory: on macOS the peak footprint of an index is about halved, -49 % on envoy, -47 % on cockroach, -48 % on vscode (#497); a warm run's peak is 30 to 40 % lower (-37 % on envoy, -40 % on terraform, -36 % on cpython) and sits at or under the cold run's (#556); the include closure of a large tree is held in an eighth of the memory, vscode's 32.5 MB of sorted vectors becoming 3.74 MB (-88 %, #505).
    • A file the nesting guard refuses no longer makes every warm run rewrite the whole cache: on envoy a 146 MB write per run is gone (#553; user CPU about 10 % lower, inside that measurement's noise).
  • Fewer false edges and more honest answers: --arch, --deps, --cochange, --impact and --export=cc.json stop reporting coupling from edges the index never had; --deps stops calling every deep graph tangled; a "not found" from an index the memory guard cut no longer reads as proof of absence; unused is printed only when the scan can prove it; a --path chain stitched from calls bound by name alone is no longer printed as the plain answer; a Rust prelude call, a macro with no in-tree binding, and a Python call to a builtin or third-party name no longer bind a same-named definition; --for reads confidence="high" on a name-exact route only when the anchor heads the rows, and hedges a second-hop caller reached only by name.
  • --for reaches more of what a task needs: registration rows and the registry key to what is registered under it (Python signals, TypeScript providers, computed-key registrars and their readers); the sites of a literal, a --flag and its default, a table or registration identifier; the family of an add-a-new-X task and the manifest lines that list a file; the doc sections for a prose question about the docs; re-export hops, alias chains and namespace re-exports; both sides of a missing path from A to B; the callers of the one function a sentence asks about; the implementations that can run at an interface call; the match arm that holds a literal; the one namesake a path word picks, while a test-only anchor among several no longer commits the route; Cargo [[bin]] and package.json bin / main entries; and <syms>, the shown files' other symbols.
  • New and changed verbs: --path and --connect hops print the call site, the call and the callee's signature; --impact sums up the importers a cut listing hides and --impact=<path> lists a file's importers; --defs prints an alias's target and --uses lists the sites written through an alias; reST and Sphinx documents become real units and --recall serves the best-matching part of an HTML page; ripwire wrap claude routes edits through a ranged Read and Claude Code's native Edit.
  • Language specifics: C++ pointers to member functions, using and namespace aliases and function-pointer typedefs; C / C++ quote includes resolve at the include roots a build passes; Rust crate::, super::, self:: and mod x; resolve in the includer's own crate; Python import a.b as c, from-imported submodule calls and src layouts; TS/JS run= runs from the package directory.
  • Contributors: a committed filename can no longer be read as commit metadata in git log framing (@pt-act, #433, #469); a TS/JS test in a subdirectory package gets a runnable run= hint (@llvm-x86, with @rainhuang0220's #330: #335, #442) and --for shows a Cargo [[bin]] and a package.json bin / main entry (@llvm-x86: #459, #460, folded into #511); langfacts slice 6c, Windows warm runs that leave the cache directory's ACL alone and shell test scripts named test-*.sh read as tests (@lennix1337: #471, #468, #496); an aliased Python import a.b as c is a file edge again, and C / C++ include roots with the foreign-qualifier veto (@jflowers: #530, #524, #526, #478); the installer hardening list of #293 (@s0undt3ch: #482); reports that led to fixes: reST and Sphinx documents (@muayyad-alsadi, #409), --for on a file name (@pguerrerolinares, #466), the router skill's language list (@gucliti, #521).
  • Upgrade: caches rebuild once (kParserVer 301, kCacheVersion 49); quality snapshots of the older scheme are not used (scheme 25).
  • Thanks: @pt-act (#433, #469), @llvm-x86 (#442, #459, #460), @lennix1337 (#436, #447, #471, #472), @jflowers (#478, #524, #526, #530), @s0undt3ch (#293), @muayyad-alsadi (#409), @pguerrerolinares (#466), @gucliti (#521) and @rainhuang0220 (#330).

Every entry of this release, with its measurement and its floors, is in CHANGELOG.md.

  • --for prints the callers of the one function a sentence asks about (SUBJECT-CALLERS-NATURAL) by @joyful-ii-V-I in https://github.com/redhat-et/ripwire/pull/527
  • Qualified endpoints: --connect counts the qualified definitions, --edit-check keeps the named owner's by @joyful-ii-V-I in https://github.com/redhat-et/ripwire/pull/528
  • --dead-code=PATH lists exported functions no non-test caller reaches, labelled apart (DEAD-CODE-SCOPE) by @joyful-ii-V-I in https://github.com/redhat-et/ripwire/pull/529
  • --path and --connect hops print the call site, the call and the callee's signature (HOP-CONTEXT) by @joyful-ii-V-I in https://github.com/redhat-et/ripwire/pull/535
  • A hop that meets an alias name continues to its target (XF-ALIAS-HOP) by @joyful-ii-V-I in https://github.com/redhat-et/ripwire/pull/537

  • perf(cache): a refused file no longer rewrites the whole cache on every warm run by @joyful-ii-V-I in https://github.com/redhat-et/ripwire/pull/553
  • --for file tail walks the file page's first 160 rows, not 40 (FOR-TAIL-WIDE) by @joyful-ii-V-I in https://github.com/redhat-et/ripwire/pull/536
  • perf(graph): hoist the by-name ladder's call-invariant reads out of the candidate loop by @joyful-ii-V-I in https://github.com/redhat-et/ripwire/pull/542
  • Python: an aliased import a.b as c records the module a.b as its Include target (#478 gap 2) by @jflowers in https://github.com/redhat-et/ripwire/pull/530
  • perf(resolve): one normalization per TS probe list, a per-build (directory, specifier) memo by @joyful-ii-V-I in https://github.com/redhat-et/ripwire/pull/554

HyperFrames v0.9.23

Released on 2026-11-08.

Fade-ins that follow a starting set now end fully visible instead of vanishing when the fade finishes. Failed script reports also name their library, and menu highlights match their panel.

Features

  • Engine: A failed CDN script's report names its library and version, never its URL (adf14d732, #7394).
  • Core: Preview health names the media element that held play (fd05a3860, #7159).

Fixes

  • Core: A set written before its tween no longer erases the tween's end (75fe18c62, #7397).
  • Studio: A menu row's highlight is rounder than its panel (ab785e9c5, #7395).
  • Studio: Bound recovery and verify full-quality playback (3151fd213, #7393).

Internal

  • Engine: The decode timeout warning reaches every caller (0f5914dc4, #7119).

HyperFrames v0.9.21

Released on 2026-11-07.

Cloud exports take big files and files given by full path, HDR clips keep their brightness, and Safari's picture meets the cut again.

Features

  • CLI: Cloud uploads send a big file in parts, so a cut connection resends one part (3d7f12acd, #7375).
  • Media Use: The voice credits line names the real source, your recording or any vendor (f5bde9533, #7380).
  • Media Use: Your own voice recording is a voice line, and speed applies to it (14e598e5a, #7377).

Fixes

  • Core: Safari video picture lands on the cut again, undoing the hidden pre-roll (841e90ffe, #7383).
  • CLI: A media file referenced by its filesystem path goes into the cloud archive (e0c11bf09, #7381).
  • Check: Lint and check skip compositions the project never mounts (7b79ab429, #7369).
  • Engine: Hlg footage with a large header sei also tone-maps at hlg's white (b559a98d3, #7349).
  • CLI: A re-encode of a media file over the cap is limited to fit it (57a75337b, #7376).
  • Core: A film moves on past a video the browser never starts loading (a595755a3, #7368).
  • Producer: Page timers during a render run on film time (59c2065d6, #7362).
  • CLI: Transcribe names why whisper is missing and the one line that fixes it (9efbbdf5a, #7366).
  • Check: Masked or clipped-away text no longer counts as overlapping (a3b8f521d, #7364).
  • Check: The overlap finding names the decorative mark, data-layout-ignore (330be7cd2, #7365).
  • CLI: Open stops the project's background preview first (896a77c30, #7297).
  • CLI: Stop waiting for the cloud's file check when the caller aborts (336abaa80, #7361).
  • Check: A Chrome that never becomes ready ends the check with a named error (e827de256, #7360).

Performance

  • Producer: Size a long video's chunks in one plan, no re-plan (c27475b97, #7382).

Internal

  • CLI: The cloud upload's PUT and retry code moves to its own file (b418bb24f, #7374).
  • Player: A Safari perf run that fails says which wait timed out and why (7b1d9cceb, #7347).

neovim/neovim ↗ 1 release · 1 prerelease
nightlyNvim development (prerelease) build Prerelease2026-11-08T05:16:13Z 
NVIM v0.13.0-dev-2052+gc868e1ab3d
Build type: RelWithDebInfo
LuaJIT 2.1.1793383622

Release notes

  • Changelog (fixes + features)
  • News (:help news in Nvim)

Install

Windows

Zip

  1. Download nvim-win64.zip (or nvim-win-arm64.zip for ARM)
  2. Extract the zip
  3. Run nvim.exe in your terminal

MSI

  1. Download nvim-win64.msi (or nvim-win-arm64.msi for ARM)
  2. Run the MSI
  3. Run nvim.exe in your terminal

Note: On Windows "Server" you may need to install vcruntime*.dll.

macOS (x86_64)

  1. Download nvim-macos-x86_64.tar.gz
  2. Run xattr -c ./nvim-macos-x86_64.tar.gz (to avoid "unknown developer" warning)
  3. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-macos-x86_64.tar.gz
  4. Run ./nvim-macos-x86_64/bin/nvim

macOS (arm64)

  1. Download nvim-macos-arm64.tar.gz
  2. Run xattr -c ./nvim-macos-arm64.tar.gz (to avoid "unknown developer" warning)
  3. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-macos-arm64.tar.gz
  4. Run ./nvim-macos-arm64/bin/nvim

Linux (x86_64)

If your system does not have the required glibc version, try the (unsupported) builds for older glibc.

AppImage

  1. Download nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage
  2. Run chmod u+x nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage && ./nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage
    • If your system does not have FUSE you can extract the appimage:
      ./nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage --appimage-extract
./squashfs-root/usr/bin/nvim

Tarball

  1. Download nvim-linux-x86_64.tar.gz
  2. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-linux-x86_64.tar.gz
  3. Run ./nvim-linux-x86_64/bin/nvim

Linux (arm64)

AppImage

  1. Download nvim-linux-arm64.appimage
  2. Run chmod u+x nvim-linux-arm64.appimage && ./nvim-linux-arm64.appimage
    • If your system does not have FUSE you can extract the appimage:
      ./nvim-linux-arm64.appimage --appimage-extract
./squashfs-root/usr/bin/nvim

Tarball

  1. Download nvim-linux-arm64.tar.gz
  2. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-linux-arm64.tar.gz
  3. Run ./nvim-linux-arm64/bin/nvim

Other

  • Install by package manager

JuliusBrussee/caveman ↗ 3 releases · 3 stable
v4.1.0Caveman 4.1.0 Stable2026-11-08T04:17:28Z

Caveman 4.1.0 is a maintenance release: bug fixes across compress, OpenCode, Hermes, Pi, the proxy and the hooks, a recovery store that no longer fills up and stops compressing, and a few small switches people asked for. caveman-compress now refuses a file with a key or token in it, and installs from skill hubs again.

Action needed

  • forward_headers under a providers.<name> block now stops the proxy from starting. Before, it was silently ignored: no headers were forwarded and the only symptom was an upstream 401. Configure that upstream as a compat mount instead: compat.<name> with base_url and forward_headers, plus wire_dialect: anthropic if it speaks the Anthropic protocol (see docs/technical/configuration.md) (#1458).
  • caveman-compress refuses a file whose text holds a credential, such as a private key block or a cloud, Git host or model API token. Compression sends the file to your model provider, so move the secret into an env var or secret manager first. The error names the line, never the secret. Placeholders like sk-... or $OPENAI_API_KEY still compress (#1471).

Changed

  • Old recovery handles can now expire. The recovery store used to keep every compressed original forever, so it filled up and compression stopped (#1370). When a write would exceed the store's cap, it now frees room: typed objects of sessions idle 14 days (archived) or 30 days (any) are expired, oldest first, until the store is at 80%. Retrieving an expired handle answers cave_expired_recovery, and each expiry is logged in proxy.log. Blob recoveries never expire (#1481).

New

  • caveman-proxy recovery-store status|check|repair for a damaged ccr.db. A damaged store now refuses writes instead of staying corrupt across upgrades; reads keep working. Repair copies only rows whose bytes still match their hash, and moves the damaged files aside untouched. caveman status and caveman doctor point at it (#1369, #1481).

  • Turn off the per-prompt reminder with CAVEMAN_REINFORCE=0, or "reinforce": false in ~/.config/caveman/config.json or a repo's .caveman.json. The full ruleset is still sent at session start (#1468).

  • --hook-node node makes installed hooks find Node through PATH at run time instead of pinning the install-time path, for people who switch Node versions (#1452). The default is unchanged, because apps started outside a terminal don't see the shell's PATH.

  • The installer falls back to Bun when Node is absent. Node stays first (#570, #1344 by @cyrus-za).

  • caveman learn scans Pi sessions, and honors PI_CODING_AGENT_DIR / PI_CODING_AGENT_SESSION_DIR (#1317 by @tranducquy0).

  • caveman mem can revert a memory to an earlier value, and supersede --stdin takes replacements too large for a command line (#1386 by @PARTHDESHMUKH2005). The client checks that the installed binary supports --stdin first, so an older binary can no longer store the literal text --stdin as the fact.

Fixed

caveman-compress

  • Hermes' skill hub blocked the install with a DANGEROUS verdict that --force can't override. Every finding was a false match, and the matched text is respelled with identical behavior (#1464).
  • Backups are keyed on the file's full path, so two projects' CLAUDE.md no longer share one backup slot. A run that dies before writing removes its backup and staging file, so the next run doesn't abort.
  • Validation now refuses commentary appended after the body (#1433). The prompt keeps the verb that carries an instruction (#1434). Frontmatter stays local on both passes (#1359 by @leninworld). Trailing punctuation on URLs and paths no longer fails validation (#1356 by @Pharex38). Credential dotfiles and bare dotfiles like .env are refused by name (#1357 by @Pharex38, #1363).

OpenCode

  • OpenCode 2: the shrink plugin is generated for the API the installed major actually loads (#1445). OPENCODE_CONFIG_DIR is honored everywhere (#1441). Deleted sessions are acted on (#1444), and the host's project directory is passed through (#1443).
  • The installer's already-installed probe reads JSONC settings.json correctly, and the plugin honors a repo defaultMode: "off" (#1430, #1431 by @Quinnod345).

Hermes

  • caveman enable hermes writes YAML that still parses (#1405). It reads ownership by value, so a reformatted config stays owned (#1410). It honors plugins.enabled / plugins.disabled membership (#1442, #1446 by @Ha1baraA11).

Pi (@caveman-ai/pi 0.3.2)

  • Per-prompt context is sent as a message, so the provider's prompt cache survives each prompt (#1364, #1376 by @worldofgeese).
  • Tool outputs under 4 KB are no longer replaced by a retrieval handle (#1365, #1378 by @danieldutu). caveman_retrieve with a query now says it returns an excerpt (#1368, #1377 by @danieldutu).
  • Pi's virtual router/auto model no longer warns that it is unroutable (#1449). The compat route is verified for an OpenAI base URL without /v1 (#1325 by @szsunyuan).

Proxy, stats and cost

  • Claude Code requests that declare the advisor tool are priced again, instead of every request showing "Unknown" cost (#1465). Streaming Anthropic responses read their outcome qualifiers from the right place (#1453).
  • A completed Codex stream closed by the client keeps its token counts (#1397, #1398 by @Ha1baraA11).
  • caveman start reads the proxy's top-level mode, not a nested middleware.mode (#1457).
  • A compat upstream blocked by the SSRF guard now answers cave_upstream_blocked and logs the host plus the CAVE_SSRF_ALLOWLIST fix, instead of a generic 502 (#1440).
  • caveman stats no longer times out on large stores: per-request cache digests moved to their own table, migrated in the background (#1371, #1481). On a timeout it names the database size and suggests --days 7.
  • Native runtime: CCR writes no longer re-scan the whole store, which in large homes made native requests run out their 250 ms budget and close empty (#1279). Overlong native.sock paths are reported (#1399, #1402 by @Ha1baraA11).
  • Recovery bytes that don't match their handle are refused (#1373 by @zsltg). The recovery store's page cap applies on every connection, and its writes are serialized (#1374, #1375 by @zsltg). Stats windows are indexed (#1372 by @zsltg). Bedrock ARN model ids keep their encoded slash (#1345 by @AlSh007). A middleware response is not marked over the limit at a quarter of it (#1367 by @AlSh007). Kimi K3 effort is mapped (#1306 by @ysntony). The wrap plugin's caveman_retrieve results are no longer recompressed (#1432).
  • Claude OAuth token refresh is relayed through the proxy. Routed Gemini pins API-key auth instead of exiting 41.
  • The pricing catalog was re-verified against vendor pages (no price changed). Vertex gemini-2.5-pro and gemini-2.5-flash were removed: Google retired both on 2026-10-20, and those requests now show as unpriced rather than mispriced (#1473).

Hooks and installer

  • Statusline setup keeps literal $ in CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR paths (#1400, #1401 by @Ha1baraA11). Managed hook entries are compared ignoring key order, so doctor --fix stops fighting a reordered entry (#1437, #1420 by @jamestrandung). Uninstall no longer deletes a settings.json.bak it didn't create (#1330 by @AlSh007).
  • Codex PostCompact hook output is valid (#1467 by @mauhpr). Cursor stops always injecting skill files from AGENTS.md (#1411 by @minhhung2556). Hook downloads are bounded and pinned to HTTPS (#1269 by @tejgokani).
  • ENABLE_TOOL_SEARCH defaults to "true" to match Claude Code (#1362 by @AliReza99).

Engine, shrink and learn

  • Escaped pipes stay inside Markdown table cells (#1456 by @kevin9327). Integers above 2^53 stay off the numeric range track (#1332 by @AlSh007). Elision notes are placed stably (#1343 by @CHLIN0). CR-separated terminal output is no longer truncated.
  • caveman-shrink skips null list entries instead of exiting (#1435, #1436 by @shaikn6), and reads Anthropic input_schema tools (#1331 by @AlSh007).
  • caveman learn stops reporting caveman's own injected context as text you pasted (#1459), and separates cold resumes from cache churn (#1419 by @jamestrandung).

Agent versions

  • Tested agent versions move up: Codex 0.166.0, Claude Code 2.1.318, Gemini CLI 0.67.0, OpenCode 1.19.5, Qwen Code 0.26.8, Kilo 7.8.27, OpenClaw 2026.11.1 and Hermes 0.23.0, each passing the repo's conformance run (#1474, with #1416 by @szsunyuan and #1451 by @anirudhqwerty). Pi stays at 1.0.4 for now; Pi 1.5.0 needs compatibility work (#1225).

Maintenance

  • Security: klauspost/compress v1.20.3 (GO-2026-6865), golang.org/x/net v0.62.0 (GO-2026-6860, GO-2026-6866), and a patched Go toolchain. @caveman-ai/middleware's MCP adapter now requires @modelcontextprotocol/sdk 1.32.0 or newer (GHSA-6prh-2h8m-c8cw), and says so when it finds an older one (#1466 by @sudip28122812-creator). filelock is patched, and the broken litellm releases are excluded (#1379 by @szsunyuan).
  • Dependency bumps: npm dev group (#1479), golang image, pip group.
  • Binaries: bin-v4.1.0. CLI: @caveman-ai/cli 4.1.0. Pi: @caveman-ai/pi 0.3.2.

Packages

  • Signed binaries, module index and container: bin-v4.1.0
  • npm CLI: @caveman-ai/cli@4.1.0
  • npm Pi extension: @caveman-ai/pi@0.3.2
  • npm middleware: @caveman-ai/middleware@1.1.0
  • PyPI middleware: caveman-middleware==1.0.1

View release →

下载

包类型 平台 说明 文件
一键安装包（推荐） Windows 7+ x64 内嵌完整运行环境，支持一键安装、自动登录与配置 QQChatExporter-Installer-v6.4.3.exe
Shell 模式 Windows x64 独立无头 (Headless) 运行环境，适合服务器或自动化部署 NapCat-QCE-Windows-x64-v6.4.3.zip
Shell 模式 Linux x64 独立无头 (Headless) 运行环境，适合服务器或 Docker 部署 NapCat-QCE-Linux-x64-v6.4.3.tar.gz
Shell 模式（预览） macOS Apple Silicon (arm64) 独立运行环境，首次启动会在本地生成一份专用的 QQ 运行副本，详见 macOS 使用指南 NapCat-QCE-macOS-arm64-v6.4.3.tar.gz
Framework 模式 Windows 作为 QQNT 插件运行，与桌面端 QQ 共存 NapCat-Framework-QCE-v6.4.3.zip
NapCat 插件商店包 跨平台 供已有 NapCat 环境的用户直接在插件商店安装 napcat-plugin-qce.zip

版本选择指南

  • 一键安装包：Windows 用户的首选。双击即可自动完成环境配置与登录（支持快捷登录或扫码），安装完毕后将自动唤起 WebUI 面板。
  • Shell 模式：独立运行版本，需单独进行 QQ 登录，适用于服务器环境部署及自动化备份需求。macOS 版本目前为预览支持，仅适用于 Apple Silicon（arm64）。
  • Framework 模式：作为 QQNT 插件运行，与当前使用的桌面端 QQ 共享登录状态，适合日常使用 Windows 桌面版 QQ 的用户。
  • NapCat 插件商店包：适用于已部署 NapCat 环境（含本地安装、Docker 或宿主机部署）的用户。请直接在 NapCat WebUI 的插件商店中搜索安装，无需手动下载此压缩包。

<details> <summary>Shell 模式使用方法</summary>

完整模式（需登录 QQ，支持导出新增聊天记录）：

  • Windows：解压后双击运行 launcher-user.bat
  • Linux：终端运行 ./launcher-user.sh
  • macOS：终端运行 ./launcher-user.sh，详见 macOS 使用指南

独立模式（无需登录 QQ，仅用于浏览已导出的记录文件）：

  • Windows：双击运行 start-standalone.bat
  • Linux：终端运行 ./start-standalone.sh
  • macOS：终端运行 ./start-standalone.sh

启动后，请在浏览器中访问 http://localhost:40653/qce。若使用完整模式，需输入控制台输出的访问令牌 (Access Token) 进行验证。

</details>

<details> <summary>Framework 模式使用方法</summary>

方式 A：通过 napiLoader.bat 运行（推荐）

此方式独立运行，无需预先安装 LiteLoaderQQNT。

  1. 解压下载的 NapCat-Framework-QCE-v6.4.3.zip 文件
  2. 确保当前已彻底退出正在运行的 QQ 客户端
  3. 双击运行 napiLoader.bat 脚本
  4. 若弹出 QQ 登录界面，请按常规方式完成登录
  5. 在浏览器中访问 http://localhost:40653/qce

方式 B：通过 LiteLoaderQQNT 加载（仅限特定需求用户）

此方式要求系统已配置好 LiteLoaderQQNT 环境。

  1. 参照 LiteLoaderQQNT 官方文档 完成前置框架的安装
  2. 打开 QQ 设置，确认左侧菜单栏已出现 LiteLoaderQQNT 选项
  3. 按照 LiteLoaderQQNT 或 NapCat 官方文档中的标准插件安装流程，将本插件部署至对应目录

<details> <summary>如何找到访问令牌？</summary>

按下 Win + R 键，输入 %USERPROFILE%\.qq-chat-exporter 并回车。打开该目录下的 security.json 文件，查找 accessToken 字段的值即可：

{
  "accessToken": "此处即为你的访问令牌",
  ...
}

</details>

</details>

<details> <summary>系统要求</summary>

需配合 QQ 客户端版本 34606 及以上使用（推荐版本 9.9.19-34740），前往下载。

  • Windows：支持 Windows 10/11 x64。如运行报错提示缺少 DLL 文件，请安装 Visual C++ 运行库。
  • Linux：支持 Ubuntu 20.04+ / Debian 11+ / CentOS 8+。内置 使用静态 musl 构建，不要求特定 glibc 版本；QQ、NapCat 和注入模块仍需满足各自的系统依赖。
  • macOS：⚠️ 预览支持，仅限 Apple Silicon（M1 及以上，arm64）。需已安装官方 macOS 版 QQ 与 Xcode 命令行工具（xcode-select --install，用于 codesign）。首次启动会在本地生成并签名一份专用的 QQ 运行副本，不会修改 /Applications/QQ.app 本体；该副本会失去系统 App 沙盒保护，仅用于后台运行 NapCat，详见 macOS 使用指南。Intel（x64）Mac 暂未提供官方安装包。

</details>

[!CAUTION] 本项目完全免费且开源，遵循 GPL-3.0 开源协议。如果您是通过付费渠道获取的本软件，请立即申请退款并进行举报。

pbakaus/impeccable ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable
cli-v4.8.1CLI 4.8.1 Stable2026-11-08T02:53:09Z

  • ES module sources. detect <dir> now scans .mjs and .cjs files, which it already checked when you named them one at a time.

  • Steadier scans. A page that floods the browser with console or network events no longer grows the process without bound, and a percent escape before a non-ASCII character no longer crashes detect.

  • Install does what you pick. A harness chosen in the interactive picker installs even when another one is already there, and the success line names every hook file it wrote.

  • Accurate update checks on Windows. check no longer reports an update on every Windows install.

  • The current detector. Engine 0.1.19 brings the contrast and background fixes in skill v4.8.1.

View release →

skill-v4.8.1Skill 4.8.1 Stable2026-11-08T02:47:12Z

  • The Stop hook asks less. Findings stay listed, and the agent fixes or skips each one with a short reason. It asks you only when it cannot decide.

  • A design hook for Hermes Agent. Hermes checks every file it writes and runs the end-of-session review before it finishes. Enable it once with hermes plugins enable impeccable-design-hook. Thanks to @kal-hermes for asking.

  • Hooks that stay wired. hooks status shows each harness's real wiring, and context restores missing entries once you have accepted the hook. A user install follows CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR, and cleanup no longer removes working project hooks. The hook stops re-reporting findings you did not touch (thanks to @provatic), VS Code Copilot hooks run under Windows PowerShell (thanks to @PhillyUrbs), and resuming a session no longer bloats the env file (thanks to @EnderWolf50).

  • Remove an element in live mode. A Remove badge on a picked element, or Delete on an empty prompt, hides it, offers Undo, and stages the removal for Apply like a copy edit.

  • Live mode fixes. Vue variants mount and keep their CSS, and Svelte and Vue previews wait for the compiled component instead of showing raw template. Tune edits survive the remaining variants arriving, the Apply repair choice survives a reload, and Rollback resets the dock (thanks to @ArmirKS for all three). Small elements pick their nearest pickable parent, captures keep self-hosted fonts, and the Nuxt plugin passes strict typecheck (thanks to @NarHakobyan).

  • Fewer false alarms. URL scans wait out brand intros and judge contrast on the ground a reader sees, under scrims, dot grids and hover effects parked off the button. Faint decorative words are advisory. A background shorthand takes its colour from its final layer (thanks to @luckystar2026), and directory scans include .mjs and .cjs files (thanks to @Mercury-nju).

  • Fixes. A harness you pick interactively installs even when another is already installed (thanks to @Minayuki523). check no longer reports updates on every Windows install (thanks to @Ibrahim4594). detect stays small on a page that floods the console (thanks to @ayagmar) and no longer crashes on a percent sign before a non-ASCII character (thanks to @Mercury-nju). Comps, review captures and critique snapshots stay out of git, and init says which files to commit (thanks to @probablykabari). New work asks before replacing an existing DESIGN.md (thanks to @MrMuzik), and a decision page left in a background tab no longer reads as closed (thanks to @connerohnesorge). Claude Code gets its own short skill description that fits its skill list.

View release →

Fixes

  • fix: skill and rule files under the agent's config dir reach the model whole; Read deny names a route that works; CTX_REPLACE_TRIGGER_BYTES setting (#1326)

  • fix: issue intake sets type labels, reads setup reports, marks same-fingerprint duplicates
